namespace NGitLab

    public readonly struct DynamicEnum<TEnum> : System.IEquatable<NGitLab.DynamicEnum<TEnum>>, System.IEquatable<TEnum>
        where TEnum :  struct, System.Enum
    {
        public DynamicEnum(TEnum enumValue) { }
        public DynamicEnum(string stringValue) { }
        public TEnum? EnumValue { get; }
        public string StringValue { get; }
        public bool Equals(NGitLab.DynamicEnum<TEnum> other) { }
        public bool Equals(TEnum other) { }
        public override bool Equals(object obj) { }
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
        public override string ToString() { }
        public static bool operator !=(NGitLab.DynamicEnum<TEnum> obj1, NGitLab.DynamicEnum<TEnum> obj2) { }
        public static bool operator !=(NGitLab.DynamicEnum<TEnum> obj1, TEnum obj2) { }
        public static bool operator ==(NGitLab.DynamicEnum<TEnum> obj1, NGitLab.DynamicEnum<TEnum> obj2) { }
        public static bool operator ==(NGitLab.DynamicEnum<TEnum> obj1, TEnum obj2) { }
    }
    public class GetCommitsRequest
    {
        public GetCommitsRequest() { }
        public bool? FirstParent { get; set; }
        public int MaxResults { get; set; }
        public string Path { get; set; }
        public uint PerPage { get; set; }
        public string RefName { get; set; }
        public System.DateTime? Since { get; set; }
        public System.DateTime? Until { get; set; }
    }
    public class GetRawFileRequest
    {
        public GetRawFileRequest() { }
        public bool? Lfs { get; init; }
        public string Ref { get; init; }
    }

    public abstract class GitLabCollectionResponse<T> : System.Collections.Generic.IAsyncEnumerable<T>, System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<T>, System.Collections.IEnumerable
    {
        protected GitLabCollectionResponse() { }
        public System.Collections.Generic.IAsyncEnumerable<T> AsAsyncEnumerable() { }
        public abstract System.Collections.Generic.IAsyncEnumerator<T> GetAsyncEnumerator(System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);
        public abstract System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerator<T> GetEnumerator();
    }
    [System.Serializable]
    public class GitLabException : System.Exception
    {
        public GitLabException() { }
        public GitLabException(string message) { }
        protected GitLabException(System.Runtime.Serialization.SerializationInfo info, System.Runtime.Serialization.StreamingContext context) { }
        public GitLabException(string message, System.Exception inner) { }
        public string ErrorMessage { get; set; }
        public System.Collections.Generic.IDictionary<string, object> ErrorObject { get; set; }
        public NGitLab.Impl.MethodType? MethodType { get; set; }
        public System.Uri OriginalCall { get; set; }
        public System.Net.HttpStatusCode StatusCode { get; set; }
    }

    public interface IHttpRequestor
    {
        void Execute(string tailAPIUrl);
        System.Threading.Tasks.Task ExecuteAsync(string tailAPIUrl, System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken);
        System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<T> GetAll<T>(string tailUrl);
        NGitLab.GitLabCollectionResponse<T> GetAllAsync<T>(string tailUrl);
        NGitLab.Models.PagedResponse<T> Page<T>(string tailAPIUrl);
        System.Threading.Tasks.Task<NGitLab.Models.PagedResponse<T>> PageAsync<T>(string tailAPIUrl, System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken);
        void Stream(string tailAPIUrl, System.Action<System.IO.Stream> parser);
        void StreamAndHeaders(string tailAPIUrl, System.Action<System.IO.Stream, System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyDictionary<string, System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<string>>> parser);
        System.Threading.Tasks.Task StreamAndHeadersAsync(string tailAPIUrl, System.Func<System.IO.Stream, System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyDictionary<string, System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<string>>, System.Threading.Tasks.Task> parser, System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken);
        System.Threading.Tasks.Task StreamAsync(string tailAPIUrl, System.Func<System.IO.Stream, System.Threading.Tasks.Task> parser, System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken);
        T To<T>(string tailAPIUrl);
        System.Threading.Tasks.Task<T> ToAsync<T>(string tailAPIUrl, System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken);
        NGitLab.IHttpRequestor With(object data);
    }

    public enum JobStatus
    {
        Unknown = 0,
        Running = 1,
        Pending = 2,
        Failed = 3,
        Success = 4,
        Created = 5,
        Canceled = 6,
        Skipped = 7,
        Manual = 8,
        NoBuild = 9,
        Preparing = 10,
        [System.Runtime.Serialization.EnumMember(Value="waiting_for_resource")]
        WaitingForResource = 11,
        Scheduled = 12,
        Canceling = 13,
    }
